### Customer First Brand Formula
_Duration: 01:55_

**Transcript:**
Discovering and creating the customer first brand model completely changed my life, and it's going to change yours too. After years trying to build online businesses, this was my key to achieving financial time and location freedom. This took me from a broke teenager fresh out of high school to becoming an 8 figure entrepreneur in just 4 years. A customer first brand is an online store that uses the drop shipping fulfillment model. And drop shipping is when you sell products through an online store without holding stock yourself. Whenever you get a sale, the supplier sends the product directly to the customer on your behalf. You connect the product with the customer, and you collect your profit in between. But where most people go wrong with this is that they use this model to sell low quality products with month long shipping times, and they just ignore customer service. They just make a store to get some quick sales before it all comes crashing down, and they have to start over again. And the customer first brand model is completely different. It's where you sell problem solving products that people actually need. You sell these products on a branded and trustworthy online store, and you use attractive and engaging video ads on social media to let people know about your products. And when they buy, you fulfill their order with the fastest shipping options available, so they wait days and not months for their order to arrive. And customer first brands, they just get better and better over time. And this challenge is all about building a store that people love, that solves a problem, and gets you your first sale of many. ## Day 4

### Finding Your Complementaries
_Duration: 01:27_

**Transcript:**
Now you have your primary product, you need 2 complementary products. Your primary product is the 1 that you will run ads to to get people to your Shopify store, and the complementary products are what you will use for up sells and bundles to increase your profit from every sale. And in this lesson, I'll show you how to find these complimentary products for your store. The first way is to just view what your competitors are selling on their store. From their product, click around their website to see what other related products they're selling to. This is the easiest and fastest way to find suitable complimentary products. The next way is to open the AliExpress page for your primary product and look at what other products are recommended on that page. While you're looking at their product listings, scroll down to see other related products that they're selling or other suppliers are offering. And the final method I'll share is to use Amazon's frequently purchased together feature. Simply find your primary product on Amazon, then scroll down the page. You'll see a section on most product pages that says what items have frequently been purchased together with that main product. Amazon has over 12000000 orders placed on the platform every day, so these recommendations are extremely valuable because of the amount of data they have to support them. Having 2 great complimentary products when you first start out is incredibly valuable. It will make you more profitable, and it will help you build a long term brand.

There is a video in the running your brand phase of the program called the troubleshooting guide. Watch that if you're in this situation because it'll walk you through what you need to do next. But for pure water so far, this data is looking good, so we're gonna continue with this product. Being able to read your data and properly troubleshoot what is going wrong is 1 of the most important skills for you as an ecommerce brand builder. This is the whole framework for reviewing your data. Look at spending money on ads, not as making money or losing money, but as investing in data. Then we can study that data and figure out what we did right and what we did wrong and use that information to improve going forward.
